index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitude and angle\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (pu)\n    Va = (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n_cost = int(gencost[i, 3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                c2 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n                c1 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 5]\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Pg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA)  # PMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA)  # PMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 1] / baseMVA)   # PG initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate complex power injection from voltage and admittance\n        S_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                # Vi * conj(Yij * Vj)\n                # Vi = Vm[i] * exp(1j*Va[i])\n                # Vj = Vm[j] * exp(1j*Va[j])\n                Y_mag = abs(Ybus[i, j])\n                Y_ang = np.angle(Ybus[i, j])\n\n                # S = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * exp(1j*(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang))\n                # Real part: Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * cos(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                # Imag part: Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * sin(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                S_calc_real = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                S_calc_imag = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n\n                S_calc += S_calc_real + 1j*S_calc_imag\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: Pg - Pd = P_calc, Qg - Qd = Q_calc\n        if isinstance(S_calc, complex):\n            g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - S_calc.real)\n            g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - S_calc.imag)\n        else:\n            # Extract real and imaginary symbolic expressions\n            # This is a symbolic calculation, extract components differently\n            pass\n\n    # Since CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # we need to compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g, h) if (g.shape[0] > 0 and h.shape[0] > 0) else (g if g.shape[0] > 0 else h)\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_load_MVAr = sum(bus[i, 3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Compute power balance check\n    max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if Vm[i] < bus[i, 12]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus[i, 12] - Vm[i])\n        elif Vm[i] > bus[i, 11]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm[i] - bus[i, 11])\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Build generator list\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen[i, 9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen[i, 8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen[i, 4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen[i, 3])\n        })\n\n    # Build bus list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(bus[i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va[i] * 180 / np.pi),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus[i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus[i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_branches[:10]\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': 'optimal' if solution['success'] else 'failed'\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(b['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(b['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': float(b['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(b['flow_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(b['flow_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': float(b['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for b in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitude and angle\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (pu)\n    Va = (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = 0\n    for i in      c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] This is a symbolic calculation, extract components differently\n            pass\n\n    # Since CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # we need to compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h = Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij =         'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_load_MVAr = sum(bus[i, 3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Compute power balance check\n    max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if Vm[i] < bus[i, 12]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus[i, 12] - Vm[i])\n        elif Vm[i] > bus[i, 11]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm[i] - bus[i, 11])\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Build generator list\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen[i, 9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen[i, 8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen[i, 4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen[i, 3])\n        })\n\n    # Build bus list\n    buses = []\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus[i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus[i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_branches[:10]\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': 'optimal' if solution['success'] else 'failed'\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(b['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(b['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': float(b['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(b['flow_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(b['flow_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': float(b['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for b in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n AC-OPF problem using CasADi and IPOPT based on math-model.md\n\"\"\"\n\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\n\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse network data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data,\n CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision reactive power\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Active power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Pg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA)  # PMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA)  # PMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 1] / baseMVA)   # PG initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        # Calculate complex power injection from voltage and admittance\n        S_calc = 0\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                # Vi * conj(Yij * Vj)\n                # Vi = Vm[i] * exp(1j*Va[i])\n                # Vj = Vm[j] * exp(1j*Va[j])\n                Y_mag = abs(Ybus[i, j])\n                Y_ang = np.angle(Ybus[i, j])\n\n                # S = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * exp(1j*(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang))\n                # Real part: Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * cos(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                # Imag part: Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * sin(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                S_calc_real = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * ca.cos(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n                S_calc_imag = Vm[i] * Vm[j] * Y_mag * ca.sin(Va[i] - Va[j] - Y_ang)\n\n                S_calc += S_calc_real + 1j*S_calc_imag\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance: Pg - Pd = P_calc, Qg - Qd = Q_calc\n        if isinstance(S_calc, complex):\n            g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - S_calc.real)\n            g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - S_calc.imag)\n        else:\n            # Extract real and imaginary symbolic expressions\n            # This is a symbolic calculation, extract components differently\n            pass\n\n    # Since CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # we need to compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g, h) if (g.shape[0] > 0 and h.shape[0] > 0) else (g if g.shape[0] > 0 else h)\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_load_MVAr = sum(bus[i, 3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Compute power balance check\n    max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if Vm[i] < bus[i, 12]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus[i, 12] - Vm[i])\n        elif Vm[i] > bus[i, 11]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm[i] - bus[i, 11])\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Build generator list\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen[i, 9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen[i, 8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen[i, 4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen[i, 3])\n        })\n\n    # Build bus list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(bus[i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va[i] * 180 / np.pi),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus[i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus[i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_branches[:10]\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': 'optimal' if solution['success'] else 'failed'\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(b['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(b['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': float(b['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(b['flow_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(b['flow_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': float(b['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for b in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = load_network_data('/root/network.json')\n    print(f\"Network: {network['n_bus']} buses, {network['n_gen']} generators, {network['n_branch']} branches\")\n\n    print(\"\\nSolving AC-OPF...\")\n    solution = solve_acopf(network)\n\n    if solution['success']:\n        print(f\"✓ Optimal solution found\")\n        print(f\"  Total cost: ${solution['cost']:.2f}/hour\")\n    else:\n        print(\"✗ Solver did not find optimal solution\")\n\n    print(\"\\nComputing branch flows...\")\n    branch_flows = compute_branch_flows(network, solution)\n\n    print(\"\\nGenerating report...\")\n    report = generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows)\n\n    with open('/root/report.json', 'w') as f:\n        json.dump(report, f, indent=2)\n\n    print(\"✓ Report saved to report.json\")\n\n    # Print summary\n    print(\"\\n\" + \"=\"*60)\n    print(\"SUMMARY\")\n    print(\"=\"*60)\n    print(f\"Total Cost:       ${report['summary']['total_cost_per_hour']:,.2f}/hour\")\n    print(f\"Total Generation: {report['summary']['total_generation_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Load:       {report['summary']['total_load_MW']:,.2f} MW\")\n    print(f\"Total Losses: constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Pg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA)  # PMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA)  # PMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 1] / baseMVA)   # PG initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    # CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # so we compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    g = ca.vertcat(*g) if g else ca.MX()\n    h = ca.vertcat(*h) if h else ca.MX()\n    constraints = ca.vertcat(g, h) if (g.shape[0] > 0 and h.shape[0] > 0) else (g if g.shape[0] > 0 else h)\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] +=        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch =  baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in 'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch =  baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch\n    }\n\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitude and angle\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (pu)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (rad)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Active power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = 0\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n_cost = int(gencost[i, 3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                c2 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n                c1 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 5]\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    # CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # so we compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_calc = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n    elif g:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*g)\n    elif h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*h)\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in     # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus   Q_calc = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n\n        # Load at bus Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift /
