in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n    elif g:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*g)\n    elif h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*h)\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series /          if r != 0 or x != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = 0\n                b_series = 0\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft -         h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen,   opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = # Compute power balance check\n    max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x) if (r != 0 or x != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else 0\n\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (rad)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Active power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n_cost = int(gencost[i, 3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                c2 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n                c1 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 5]\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * ubx.append(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA)  # PMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 1] / baseMVA)   # PG initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # 'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji =     Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if Vm[i] < bus[i, 12]:\n            max_v_violation = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Build generator list\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n json\nimport numpy as np\nimport casadi as ca\n\n\ndef load_network_data(filename):\n    \"\"\"Load and parse network data from JSON file.\"\"\"\n    with open(filename, 'r') as f:\n        data = json.load(f)\n\n    baseMVA = data['baseMVA']\n\n    # Parse bus data\n    bus_data = np.array(data['bus'])\n    n_bus = len(bus_data)\n\n    # Parse generator data\n    gen_data = np.array(data['gen'])\n    gencost_data = np.array(data['gencost'])\n    n_gen = len(gen_data)\n\n    # Parse branch data\n    branch_data = np.array(data['branch'])\n    n_branch = n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch\n    }\n\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage magnitude and angle\n    Vm = ca.MX.sym('Vm', n_bus)  # Voltage magnitude (pu)\n    Va = ca.MX.sym('Va', n_bus)  # Voltage angle (rad)\n\n    # Generator active and reactive power\n    Pg = ca.MX.sym('Pg', n_gen)  # Active power (pu)\n    Qg = ca.MX.sym('Qg', n_gen)  # Reactive power (pu)\n\n    # Collect all variables\n    x = ca.vertcat(Vm, Va, Pg, Qg)\n\n    # ========== Objective Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n_cost = int(gencost[i, 3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                c2 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n                c1 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 5]\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage angle bounds and reference bus constraint\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if bus[i, 1] == 3:  # Slack bus\n            lbx.append(0)\n            ubx.append(0)\n            x0.append(0)\n        else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        # Pg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA)  # PMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA)  # PMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 1] / baseMVA)   # PG initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG initial\n\n    # ========== Power Balance Constraints ==========\n    # For each bus: sum(Sg) - sum(Sd) - Yshunt*|V|^2 = sum(Sij)\n\n    # Build admittance matrix\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    # CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # so we compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_calc = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x_br = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x_br != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = ca.MX(0)\n                b_series = ca.MX(0)\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n    elif g:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*g)\n    elif h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*h)\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 3000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x_br != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji =     Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = {"type":"user","message":{"role":"user","content":[{"type":"text","text":"Base directory for this skill: /logs/agent/sessions/skills/ac-branch-pi-model\n\n# AC Branch Pi-Model + Transformer Handling\n\nImplement the exact branch power flow equations in `acopf-math-model.md` using MATPOWER branch data:\n\n```\n[F_BUS, T_BUS, BR_R, BR_X, BR_B, RATE_A, RATE_B, RATE_C, TAP, SHIFT, BR_STATUS, ANGMIN, ANGMAX]\n```\n\n## Quick start\n\n- Use `scripts/branch_flows.py` to compute per-unit branch flows.\n- Treat the results as **power leaving** the “from” bus and **power leaving** the “to” bus (i.e., compute both directions explicitly).\n\nExample:\n\n```python\nimport json\nimport numpy as np\n\nfrom scripts.branch_flows import compute_branch_flows_pu, build_bus_id_to_idx\n\ndata = json.load(open(\"/root/network.json\"))\nbaseMVA = float(data[\"baseMVA\"])\nbuses = np.array(data[\"bus\"], dtype=float)\nbranches = np.array(data[\"branch\"], dtype=float)\n\nbus_id_to_idx = build_bus_id_to_idx(buses)\n\nVm = buses[:, 7]  # initial guess VM\nVa = np.deg2rad(buses[:, 8])  # initial guess VA\n\nbr = branches[0]\nP_ij, Q_ij, P_ji, Q_ji = compute_branch_flows_pu(Vm, Va, br, bus_id_to_idx)\n\nS_ij_MVA = (P_ij**2 + Q_ij**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nS_ji_MVA = (P_ji**2 + Q_ji**2) ** 0.5 * baseMVA\nprint(S_ij_MVA, S_ji_MVA)\n```\n\n## Model details (match the task formulation)\n\n### Per-unit conventions\n\n- Work in per-unit internally.\n- Convert with `baseMVA`:\n  - \\(P_{pu} = P_{MW} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(Q_{pu} = Q_{MVAr} / baseMVA\\)\n  - \\(|S|_{MVA} = |S|_{pu} \\cdot baseMVA\\)\n\n### Transformer handling (MATPOWER TAP + SHIFT)\n\n- Use \\(T_{ij} = tap \\cdot e^{j \\cdot shift}\\).\n- **Implementation shortcut (real tap + phase shift):**\n  - If `abs(TAP) < 1e-12`, treat `tap = 1.0` (no transformer).\n  - Convert `SHIFT` from degrees to radians.\n  - Use the angle shift by modifying the angle difference:\n    - \\(\\delta_{ij} = \\theta_i - \\theta_j - shift\\)\n    - \\(\\delta_{ji} = \\theta_j - \\theta_i + shift\\)\n\n### Series admittance\n\nGiven `BR_R = r`, `BR_X = x`:\n\n- If `r == 0 and x == 0`, set `g = 0`, `b = 0` (avoid divide-by-zero).\n- Else:\n  - \\(y = 1/(r + jx) = g + jb\\)\n  - \\(g = r/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n  - \\(b = -x/(r^2 + x^2)\\)\n\n### Line charging susceptance\n\n- `BR_B` is the **total** line charging susceptance \\(b_c\\) (per unit).\n- Each end gets \\(b_c/2\\) in the standard pi model.\n\n## Power flow equations (use these exactly)\n\nLet:\n- \\(V_i = |V_i| e^{j\\theta_i}\\), \\(V_j = |V_j| e^{j\\theta_j}\\)\n- `tap` is real, `shift` is radians\n- `inv_t = 1/tap`, `inv_t2 = inv_t^2`\n\nThen the real/reactive power flow from i→j is:\n\n- \\(P_{ij} = g |V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ij} + b\\sin\\delta_{ij})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ij} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_i|^2 inv\\_t2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ij} - b\\cos\\delta_{ij})\\)\n\nAnd from j→i is:\n\n- \\(P_{ji} = g |V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\cos\\delta_{ji} + b\\sin\\delta_{ji})\\)\n- \\(Q_{ji} = -(b + b_c/2)|V_j|^2 - |V_i||V_j| inv\\_t (g\\sin\\delta_{ji} - b\\cos\\delta_{ji})\\)\n\nCompute apparent power:\n\n- \\(|S_{ij}| = \\sqrt{P_{ij}^2 + Q_{ij}^2}\\)\n- \\(|S_{ji}| = \\sqrt{P_{ji}^2 + Q_{ji}^2}\\)\n\n## Common uses\n\n### Enforce MVA limits (rateA)\n\n- `RATE_A` is an MVA limit (may be 0 meaning “no limit”).\n- Enforce in both directions:\n  - \\(|S_{ij}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n  - \\(|S_{ji}| \\le RATE_A\\)\n\n### Compute branch loading %\n\nFor reporting “most loaded branches”:\n\n- `loading_pct = 100 * max(|S_ij|, |S_ji|) / RATE_A` if `RATE_A > 0`, else 0.\n\n### Aggregate bus injections for nodal balance\n\nTo build the branch flow sum for each bus \\(i\\):\n\n- Add \\(P_{ij}, Q_{ij}\\) to bus i\n- Add \\(P_{ji}, Q_{ji}\\) to bus j\n\nThis yields arrays `P_out[i]`, `Q_out[i]` such that the nodal balance can be written as:\n\n- \\(P^g - P^d - G^s|V|^2 = P_{out}\\)\n- \\(Q^g - Q^d + B^s|V|^2 = Q_{out}\\)\n\n## Sanity checks (fast debug)\n\n- With `SHIFT=0` and `TAP=1`, if \\(V_i = V_j\\) and \\(\\theta_i=\\theta_j\\), then \\(P_{ij}\\approx 0\\) and \\(P_{ji}\\approx 0\\) (lossless only if r=0).\n- For a pure transformer (`r=x=0`) you should not get meaningful flows; treat as `g=b=0` (no series element).\n"}]},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"fdc85159-8e5c-487c-97d9-f7fd3d1723d3","uuid":"8cfec372-71cf-4658-b65f-56566ccb5317","isSynthetic":true}

0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create generator bus mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    # CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # so we compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_calc = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x_br = branch[k, 3]\n            b = branch[k, 4]\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance\n            if r != 0 or x_br != 0:\n                y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n                g_series = y_series.real\n                b_series = y_series.imag\n            else:\n                g_series = ca.MX(0)\n                b_series = ca.MX(0)\n\n            # Shunt admittance\n            b_shunt = b / 2\n\n            # From-side power flow\n            # Sij = (Yij + Yc_ij)^* * |Vi|^2 / |tap|^2 - Yij^* * Vi * Vj^* / tap\n            c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n            s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n            # From end total admittance\n            g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n            b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n            # Mutual admittance accounting for tap and shift\n            g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n            b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n            # Active and reactive power from bus i to j\n            Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + b_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n            Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*ca.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - b_tf_mutual*ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n    elif g:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*g)\n    elif h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*h)\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Improve initial guess - scale up generation to match load + losses estimate\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    total_load = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(network['n_bus']))\n    total_gen_init = sum(gen[i, 1] for i in range(network['n_gen']) if gen[i, 7] == 1)\n\n    # Estimate 2% losses\n    target_gen = total_load * 1.02\n    scale_factor = target_gen / total_gen_init if total_gen_init > 0 else 1.5\n\n    # Scale generator initial points\n    for i in range(network['n_gen']):\n        idx = 2 * network['n_bus'] + i\n        x0[idx] = min(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA, max(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA, x0[idx] * scale_factor))\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver with relaxed tolerances\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 5000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_bound_push': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.warm_start_mult_bound_push': 1e-6,\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x_br != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Build Ybus\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    Ybus = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Generator mapping\n    gen_at_bus = [[] for _ in range(n_bus)]\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        bus_i = bus_idx[bus_num]\n        gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    max_p_mismatch = 0\n    max_q_mismatch = 0\n\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = 0\n        Q_calc = 0\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * np.sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        p_mismatch = abs((Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc) * baseMVA)\n        q_mismatch = abs((Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc) * baseMVA)\n\n        max_p_mismatch = max(max_p_mismatch, p_mismatch)\n        max_q_mismatch = max(max_q_mismatch, q_mismatch)\n\n    return max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch\n\n\ndef generate_report(network, solution, branch_flows):\n    \"\"\"Generate the final report in JSON format.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = solution['Pg']\n    Qg = solution['Qg']\n\n    # Compute totals\n    total_gen_MW = sum(Pg) * baseMVA\n    total_gen_MVAr = sum(Qg) * baseMVA\n\n    total_load_MW = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(n_bus))\n    total_load_MVAr = sum(bus[i, 3] for i in range(n_bus))\n\n    total_losses_MW = total_gen_MW - total_load_MW\n\n    # Compute power balance check\n    max_p_mismatch, max_q_mismatch = compute_power_balance_check(network, solution)\n\n    # Check voltage violations\n    max_v_violation = 0\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        if Vm[i] < bus[i, 12]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, bus[i, 12] - Vm[i])\n        elif Vm[i] > bus[i, 11]:\n            max_v_violation = max(max_v_violation, Vm[i] - bus[i, 11])\n\n    # Check branch overloads\n    max_overload = 0\n    for bf in branch_flows:\n        if bf['limit_MVA'] > 0:\n            overload = max(0, bf['flow_from_MVA'] - bf['limit_MVA'])\n            max_overload = max(max_overload, overload)\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Build generator list\n    generators = []\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        bus_num = int(gen[i, 0])\n        generators.append({\n            'id': i + 1,\n            'bus': bus_num,\n            'pg_MW': float(Pg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'qg_MVAr': float(Qg[i] * baseMVA),\n            'pmin_MW': float(gen[i, 9]),\n            'pmax_MW': float(gen[i, 8]),\n            'qmin_MVAr': float(gen[i, 4]),\n            'qmax_MVAr': float(gen[i, 3])\n        })\n\n    # Build bus list\n    buses = []\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        bus_num = int(bus[i, 0])\n        buses.append({\n            'id': bus_num,\n            'vm_pu': float(Vm[i]),\n            'va_deg': float(Va[i] * 180 / np.pi),\n            'vmin_pu': float(bus[i, 12]),\n            'vmax_pu': float(bus[i, 11])\n        })\n\n    # Get most loaded branches\n    sorted_branches = sorted(branch_flows, key=lambda x: x['loading_pct'], reverse=True)\n    most_loaded = sorted_branches[:10]\n\n    report = {\n        'summary': {\n            'total_cost_per_hour': float(solution['cost']),\n            'total_load_MW': float(total_load_MW),\n            'total_load_MVAr': float(total_load_MVAr),\n            'total_generation_MW': float(total_gen_MW),\n            'total_generation_MVAr': float(total_gen_MVAr),\n            'total_losses_MW': float(total_losses_MW),\n            'solver_status': 'optimal' if solution['success'] else 'failed'\n        },\n        'generators': generators,\n        'buses': buses,\n        'most_loaded_branches': [\n            {\n                'from_bus': int(b['from_bus']),\n                'to_bus': int(b['to_bus']),\n                'loading_pct': float(b['loading_pct']),\n                'flow_from_MVA': float(b['flow_from_MVA']),\n                'flow_to_MVA': float(b['flow_to_MVA']),\n                'limit_MVA': float(b['limit_MVA'])\n            }\n            for b in most_loaded\n        ],\n        'feasibility_check': {\n            'max_p_mismatch_MW': float(max_p_mismatch),\n            'max_q_mismatch_MVAr': float(max_q_mismatch),\n            'max_voltage_violation_pu': float(max_v_violation),\n            'max_branch_overload_MVA': float(max_overload)\n        }\n    }\n\n    return report\n\n\ndef main():\n    \"\"\"Main execution function.\"\"\"\n    print(\"Loading network data...\")\n    network = solution = solve_acopf(network)\n\n    if solution['success']:\n        print(f\"✓ Optimal solution found\")\n        print(f\"  Total cost: ${solution['cost']:.2f}/hour\")\n    else:\n  n_branch = len(branch_data)\n\n    return {\n        'baseMVA': baseMVA,\n        'bus': bus_data,\n        'gen': gen_data,\n        'gencost': gencost_data,\n        'branch': branch_data,\n        'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch\n    }\n\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    gencost = network['gencost']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage Function ==========\n    # Cost function: sum of c2*Pg^2 + c1*Pg + c0\n    cost = ca.MX(0)\n    for i in range(n_gen):\n        if gencost[i, 0] == 2:  # Polynomial cost\n            n_cost = int(gencost[i, 3])\n            if n_cost == 3:  # Quadratic cost\n                c2 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c1 = gencost[i, 5]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 6]\n                # Convert from pu to MW for cost calculation\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c2 * Pg_MW**2 + c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n            elif n_cost == 2:  # Linear cost\n                c1 = gencost[i, 4]\n                c0 = gencost[i, 5]\n                Pg_MW = Pg[i] * baseMVA\n                cost += c1 * Pg_MW + c0\n\n    # ========== Constraints ==========\n    g = []  # Equality constraints\n    lbg = []\n    ubg = []\n    h = []  # Inequality constraints\n    lbh = []\n    ubh = []\n\n    # Variable bounds\n    lbx = []\n    ubx = []\n    x0 = []  # Initial guess\n\n    # Voltage magnitude bounds\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        lbx.append(bus[i, 12])  # VMIN\n        ubx.append(bus[i, 11])  # VMAX\n        x0.append(bus[i, 7])    # VM initial\n\n    # Voltage else:\n            lbx.append(-np.pi)\n            ubx.append(np.pi)\n            x0.append(bus[i, 8] * np.pi / 180)  # VA initial in radians\n\n    # Generator power bounds\n    for i in initial\n\n        # Qg bounds (convert to pu)\n        lbx.append(gen[i, 4] / baseMVA)  # QMIN\n        ubx.append(gen[i, 3] / baseMVA)  # QMAX\n        x0.append(gen[i, 2] / baseMVA)   # QG dtype=complex)\n\n    # Add branch contributions\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n\n        # Series admittance\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n\n        # Shunt admittance\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n\n        # Transformer ratio\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        # Add to Ybus (pi-model)\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    # Add shunt admittances from bus data\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        Gs = bus[i, 4]  # Shunt conductance\n        Bs = bus[i, 5]  # Shunt susceptance\n        Ybus[i, i] += Gs + 1j*Bs\n\n    # Create gen_at_bus[bus_i].append(i)\n\n    # Power balance equations for each bus\n    # CasADi doesn't support complex arithmetic directly,\n    # so we compute real and imaginary parts separately\n    for i in range(n_bus):\n        P_calc = ca.MX(0)\n        Q_calc = ca.MX(0)\n\n        for j in range(n_bus):\n            if Ybus[i, j] != 0:\n                G_ij = Ybus[i, j].real\n                B_ij = Ybus[i, j].imag\n\n                # P_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * cos(Va[i]-Va[j]) + B_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n                # Q_calc += Vm[i] * Vm[j] * (G_ij * sin(Va[i]-Va[j]) - B_ij * ca.cos(Va[i]-Va[j]))\n\n        # Generator injection at bus i\n        Pg_bus = sum(Pg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n        Qg_bus = sum(Qg[k] for k in gen_at_bus[i]) if gen_at_bus[i] else ca.MX(0)\n\n        # Load at bus i (convert to pu)\n        Pd = bus[i, 2] / baseMVA\n        Qd = bus[i, 3] / baseMVA\n\n        # Power balance equations\n        g.append(Pg_bus - Pd - P_calc)\n        g.append(Qg_bus - Qd - Q_calc)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n        lbg.append(0)\n        ubg.append(0)\n\n    # ========== Branch Flow Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Skip out-of-service branches\n            continue\n\n        if branch[k, 5] > 0:  # RATE_A (MVA limit)\n            f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n            t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n            i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n            j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n            r = branch[k, 2]\n            x_br = branch[k, 3]\n            bc = branch[k, 4]  # Total line charging\n            tap = branch[k, 8] if abs(branch[k, 8]) > 1e-12 else 1.0\n            shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n            # Series admittance: y = 1/(r+jx) = g + jb\n            if abs(r) < 1e-12 and abs(x_br) < 1e-12:\n                g = 0.0\n                b = 0.0\n            else:\n                denom = r**2 + x_br**2\n                g = r / denom\n                b = -x_br / denom\n\n            inv_t = 1.0 / tap\n            inv_t2 = inv_t**2\n\n            # From i->j: use correct formula from ac-branch-pi-model\n            delta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j] - shift\n            Pij = g * Vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*inv_t*(g*ca.cos(delta_ij) + b*ca.sin(delta_ij))\n            Qij = -(b + bc/2) * Vm[i]**2 * inv_t2 - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*inv_t*(g*ca.sin(delta_ij) - b*ca.cos(delta_ij))\n\n            # Apparent power squared\n            Sij_sq = Pij**2 + Qij**2\n\n            # Limit in pu\n            S_limit_pu = (branch[k, 5] / baseMVA)**2\n\n            h.append(Sij_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n            # Also check j->i direction\n            delta_ji = Va[j] - Va[i] + shift\n            Pji = g * Vm[j]**2 - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*inv_t*(g*ca.cos(delta_ji) + b*ca.sin(delta_ji))\n            Qji = -(b + bc/2) * Vm[j]**2 - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*inv_t*(g*ca.sin(delta_ji) - b*ca.cos(delta_ji))\n\n            Sji_sq = Pji**2 + Qji**2\n            h.append(Sji_sq)\n            lbh.append(0)\n            ubh.append(S_limit_pu)\n\n    # ========== Angle Difference Limits ==========\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        ang_min = branch[k, 11] * np.pi / 180  # Convert to radians\n        ang_max = branch[k, 12] * np.pi / 180\n\n        if ang_min != -360*np.pi/180 or ang_max != 360*np.pi/180:\n            h.append(Va[i] - Va[j])\n            lbh.append(ang_min)\n            ubh.append(ang_max)\n\n    # Stack constraints\n    if g and h:\n        g_vec = ca.vertcat(*g)\n        h_vec = ca.vertcat(*h)\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(g_vec, h_vec)\n    elif g:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*g)\n    elif h:\n        constraints = ca.vertcat(*h)\n    else:\n        constraints = ca.MX()\n\n    lbg_all = lbg + lbh\n    ubg_all = ubg + ubh\n\n    # Create NLP\n    nlp = {\n        'x': x,\n        'f': cost,\n        'g': constraints\n    }\n\n    return nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg_all, ubg_all, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx\n\n\ndef solve_acopf(network):\n    \"\"\"Solve the AC-OPF problem.\"\"\"\n\n    nlp, lbx, ubx, x0, lbg, ubg, n_bus, n_gen, bus_idx = build_acopf_model(network)\n\n    # Improve initial guess - scale up generation to match load + losses estimate\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    total_load = sum(bus[i, 2] for i in range(network['n_bus']))\n    total_gen_init = sum(gen[i, 1] for i in range(network['n_gen']) if gen[i, 7] == 1)\n\n    # Estimate 2% losses\n    target_gen = total_load * 1.02\n    scale_factor = target_gen / total_gen_init if total_gen_init > 0 else 1.5\n\n    # Scale generator initial points\n    for i in range(network['n_gen']):\n        idx = 2 * network['n_bus'] + i\n        x0[idx] = min(gen[i, 8] / baseMVA, max(gen[i, 9] / baseMVA, x0[idx] * scale_factor))\n\n    # Create IPOPT solver with relaxed tolerances\n    opts = {\n        'ipopt.print_level': 5,\n        'ipopt.max_iter': 5000,\n        'ipopt.tol': 1e-4,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_tol': 1e-3,\n        'ipopt.acceptable_iter': 15,\n        'ipopt.mu_strategy': 'adaptive',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_init_point': 'yes',\n        'ipopt.warm_start_bound_push': 1e-6,\n        'ipopt.warm_start_mult_bound_push': 1e-6,\n        'print_time': False\n    }\n\n    solver = ca.nlpsol('solver', 'ipopt', nlp, opts)\n\n    # Solve\n    sol = solver(x0=x0, lbx=lbx, ubx=ubx, lbg=lbg, ubg=ubg)\n\n    # Extract solution\n    x_opt = sol['x'].full().flatten()\n\n    # Parse solution\n    Vm_opt = x_opt[0:n_bus]\n    Va_opt = x_opt[n_bus:2*n_bus]\n    Pg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus:2*n_bus+n_gen]\n    Qg_opt = x_opt[2*n_bus+n_gen:2*n_bus+2*n_gen]\n\n    # Get solver status\n    stats = solver.stats()\n    success = stats['success']\n\n    return {\n        'Vm': Vm_opt,\n        'Va': Va_opt,\n        'Pg': Pg_opt,\n        'Qg': Qg_opt,\n        'cost': float(sol['f']),\n        'success': success,\n        'stats': stats\n    }\n\n\ndef compute_branch_flows(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Compute branch power flows and loadings.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    branch = network['branch']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(network['n_bus'])}\n\n    branch_flows = []\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:  # Out of service\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n        i = bus_idx[f_bus]\n        j = bus_idx[t_bus]\n\n        r = branch[k, 2]\n        x_br = branch[k, 3]\n        b = branch[k, 4]\n        tap = branch[k, 8] if branch[k, 8] != 0 else 1.0\n        shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n        rate_a = branch[k, 5]\n\n        # Series admittance\n        if r != 0 or x_br != 0:\n            y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br)\n            g_series = y_series.real\n            b_series = y_series.imag\n        else:\n            g_series = 0\n            b_series = 0\n\n        b_shunt = b / 2\n\n        # From-side flow\n        c_shift = np.cos(shift)\n        s_shift = np.sin(shift)\n\n        g_ft = g_series / (tap**2)\n        b_ft = (b_series + b_shunt) / (tap**2)\n\n        g_tf_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap + b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_tf_mutual = -g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        theta_ij = Va[i] - Va[j]\n\n        Pij = Vm[i]**2 * g_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij) + b_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij))\n        Qij = -Vm[i]**2 * b_ft - Vm[i]*Vm[j]*(g_tf_mutual*np.sin(theta_ij) - b_tf_mutual*np.cos(theta_ij))\n        Sij = np.sqrt(Pij**2 + Qij**2)\n\n        # To-side flow\n        g_tf = g_series\n        b_tf = b_series + b_shunt\n\n        g_ft_mutual = g_series * c_shift / tap - b_series * s_shift / tap\n        b_ft_mutual = g_series * s_shift / tap + b_series * c_shift / tap\n\n        Pji = Vm[j]**2 * g_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij) + b_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij))\n        Qji = -Vm[j]**2 * b_tf - Vm[j]*Vm[i]*(g_ft_mutual*np.sin(-theta_ij) - b_ft_mutual*np.cos(-theta_ij))\n        Sji = np.sqrt(Pji**2 + Qji**2)\n\n        # Convert to MVA\n        Sij_MVA = Sij * baseMVA\n        Sji_MVA = Sji * baseMVA\n\n        # Calculate loading percentage\n        loading_pct = (Sij_MVA / rate_a * 100) if rate_a > 0 else 0\n\n        branch_flows.append({\n            'from_bus': f_bus,\n            'to_bus': t_bus,\n            'flow_from_MVA': Sij_MVA,\n            'flow_to_MVA': Sji_MVA,\n            'limit_MVA': rate_a,\n            'loading_pct': loading_pct\n        })\n\n    return branch_flows\n\n\ndef compute_power_balance_check(network, solution):\n    \"\"\"Verify power balance constraints.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA = network['baseMVA']\n    bus = network['bus']\n    gen = network['gen']\n    n_bus = network['n_bus']\n    n_gen = network['n_gen']\n\n    Vm = solution['Vm']\n    Va = solution['Va']\n    Pg = np.zeros((n_bus, n_bus), dtype=complex)\n\n    for k in range(n_branch):\n        if branch[k, 10] == 0:\n            continue\n\n        f_bus = int(branch[k, 0])\n        t_bus = int(branch[k, 1])\n    shift = branch[k, 9] * np.pi / 180\n\n        y_series = 1 / (r + 1j*x_br) if (r != 0 or x_br != 0) else 0\n        y_shunt = 1j * b / 2\n        t_ratio = tap * np.exp(1j * shift)\n\n        Ybus[i, i] += (y_series + y_shunt) / (tap**2)\n        Ybus[j, j] += y_series + y_shunt\n        Ybus[i, j] += -y_series / np.conj(t_ratio)\n        Ybus[j, i] += -y_series / t_ratio\n\n    for i in 'n_bus': n_bus,\n        'n_gen': n_gen,\n        'n_branch': n_branch\n    }\n\n\ndef build_acopf_model(network):\n    \"\"\"Build the AC-OPF optimization model using CasADi.\"\"\"\n\n    baseMVA =  n_branch = network['n_branch']\n\n    # Create bus index mapping (bus number -> index)\n    bus_idx = {int(bus[i, 0]): i for i in range(n_bus)}\n\n    # Decision variables\n    # Bus voltage
